# Third-party licenses

The following sets forth attribution notices for third-party software included in DevKit, one entry per
package with the license text its authors published. It is generated by
`bun scripts/third-party-licenses.mjs` — do not edit it by hand.

It covers what is linked or bundled into the app: Go modules (the app and the TypeScript analyzer), npm
packages bundled into the interface, the Java analyzer's runtime libraries and the Inter font. The Claude
and Codex agent runtimes are downloaded on your machine when you install them from Settings → Agents;
they are not part of DevKit and keep their own licenses.

552 packages.

## Go modules

# Jackson JSON processor

Jackson is a high-performance, Free/Open Source JSON processing library.
It was originally written by Tatu Saloranta (tatu.saloranta@iki.fi), and has
been in development since 2007.
It is currently developed by a community of developers.

Copyright 2007-, Tatu Saloranta (tatu.saloranta@iki.fi)

in some artifacts (usually source distributions); but is always available
from the source code management (SCM) system project uses.

## FastDoubleParser

jackson-core bundles a shaded copy of FastDoubleParser <https://github.com/wrandelshofer/FastDoubleParser>.
That code is available under an MIT license <https://github.com/wrandelshofer/FastDoubleParser/blob/main/LICENSE>
under the following copyright.

Copyright © 2023 Werner Randelshofer, Switzerland. MIT License.

See FastDoubleParser-NOTICE for details of other source code included in FastDoubleParser
and the licenses and copyrights that apply to that code.
